Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on stratigraphy structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 572. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 612 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the stratigraphy space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 572 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 612. QED.
